## 回答
以下是完善后的代码，添加了中文注释，便于理解代码的功能和逻辑：

```python
import os
import cv2
import numpy as np
import onnxruntime as ort

class SAM2ImagePredictor:
    def __init__(self, encoder_model_path, decoder_model_path):
        """
        初始化 SAM2 图像分割预测器
        :param encoder_model_path: 编码器模型路径
        :param decoder_model_path: 解码器模型路径
        """
        self.encoder_session = ort.InferenceSession(encoder_model_path)
        self.decoder_session = ort.InferenceSession(decoder_model_path)

    def trunc_normal(self, size, std=0.02, a=-2, b=2):
        """
        生成截断正态分布的随机数
        :param size: 输出数组的形状
        :param std: 标准差
        :param a: 截断下限
        :param b: 截断上限
        :return: 截断正态分布的随机数数组
        """
        values = np.random.normal(loc=0., scale=std, size=size)
        values = np.clip(values, a * std, b * std)
        return values

    def set_image(self, image):
        """
        设置输入图像并获取编码器的特征
        :param image: 输入图像
        :return: 编码器的特征
        """
        image = np.expand_dims(image, axis=0).astype(np.float32)
        outputs = self.encoder_session.run(None, {"input_image": image})
        high_res_feats_0, high_res_feats_1, image_embed = outputs
        features = {
            "high_res_feats_0": high_res_feats_0,
            "high_res_feats_1": high_res_feats_1,
            "image_embed": image_embed
        }
        return features

    def predict(self, features, orig_hw, point_coords=None, point_labels=None, box=None, mask_input=None):
        """
        进行图像分割预测
        :param features: 编码器的特征
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :param point_coords: 点提示的坐标
        :param point_labels: 点提示的标签
        :param box: 边框提示
        :param mask_input: 掩码输入
        :return: 分割掩码、IoU 预测值和低分辨率掩码
        """
        if point_coords is not None and len(point_coords) != 0:
            point_coords = point_coords.astype(np.float32)
            unnorm_coords = self.transform_coords(point_coords, orig_hw)
            labels = point_labels.astype(np.int64)
            if len(unnorm_coords.shape) == 2:
                unnorm_coords, labels = unnorm_coords[None, ...], labels[None, ...]
        else:
            unnorm_coords, labels = None, None

        if box is not None:
            box = box.astype(np.float32)
            unnorm_box = self.transform_boxes(box, orig_hw)
        else:
            unnorm_box = None

        if mask_input is not None:
            mask_input = mask_input.astype(np.float32)
            if len(mask_input.shape) == 3:
                mask_input = mask_input[None, :, :, :]
        else:
            mask_input = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)

        if unnorm_coords is not None:
            concat_points = (unnorm_coords, labels)
        else:
            concat_points = None

        if unnorm_box is not None:
            box_coords = unnorm_box.reshape(-1, 2, 2)
            box_labels = np.array([[2, 3]])
            box_labels = box_labels.repeat(unnorm_box.shape[0], 1)
            if concat_points is not None:
                concat_coords = np.concatenate([box_coords, concat_points[0]], axis=1)
                concat_labels = np.concatenate([box_labels, concat_points[1]], axis=1)
                concat_points = (concat_coords, concat_labels.astype(np.int32))
            else:
                concat_points = (box_coords, box_labels.astype(np.int32))

        if mask_input is None:
            mask_input_dummy = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)
            masks_enable = np.array([0], dtype=np.int32)
        else:
            mask_input_dummy = mask_input
            masks_enable = np.array([1], dtype=np.int32)

        if concat_points is None:
            raise ValueError("concat_points must be exists")

        sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings, dense_pe = self.decoder_session.run(
            None,
            {
                "coords": concat_points[0],
                "labels": concat_points[1],
                "masks": mask_input_dummy,
                "masks_enable": masks_enable,
                "image_embeddings": features["image_embed"],
                "high_res_features1": features["high_res_feats_0"],
                "high_res_features2": features["high_res_feats_1"]
            }
        )

        masks, iou_pred = sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings

        low_res_masks = masks[:, 1:, :, :]
        iou_predictions = iou_pred[:, 1:]

        masks = self.postprocess_masks(masks, orig_hw)

        return masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks

    def transform_coords(self, coords, orig_hw):
        """
        转换坐标
        :param coords: 坐标
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 转换后的坐标
        """
        h, w = orig_hw
        coords = coords.copy()
        coords[..., 0] = coords[..., 0] / w
        coords[..., 1] = coords[..., 1] / h

        resolution = 1024
        coords = coords * resolution
        return coords

    def transform_boxes(self, boxes, orig_hw):
        """
        转换边框
        :param boxes: 边框
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 转换后的边框
        """
        boxes = self.transform_coords(boxes.reshape(-1, 2, 2), orig_hw)
        return boxes

    def postprocess_masks(self, masks, orig_hw):
        """
        后处理分割掩码
        :param masks: 分割掩码
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 后处理后的分割掩码
        """
        interpolated_masks = []
        for mask in masks:
            mask = np.transpose(mask, (1, 2, 0))
            resized_mask = cv2.resize(mask, (orig_hw[1], orig_hw[0]), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
            resized_mask = np.transpose(resized_mask, (2, 0, 1))
            interpolated_masks.append(resized_mask)
        interpolated_masks = np.array(interpolated_masks)

        return interpolated_masks


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 输入图片路径
    input_image_path = "../assets/rgb1.png"
    # 输出图片路径
    output_image_path = "../result/segment_onnx.png"
    # 编码器模型路径
    encoder_model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.encoder.onnx"
    # 解码器模型路径
    decoder_model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.decoder.onnx"

    # 读取输入图片
    image = cv2.imread(input_image_path)
    image = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
    orig_hw = image.shape[:2]

    # 初始化 SAM2 图像分割预测器
    predictor = SAM2ImagePredictor(encoder_model_path, decoder_model_path)

    # 设置输入图像并获取编码器的特征
    features = predictor.set_image(image)

    # 进行图像分割预测
    masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks = predictor.predict(features, orig_hw)

    # 将分割掩码转换为可视化图像
    mask = masks[0, 0].astype(np.uint8) * 255
    mask = cv2.cvtColor(mask, cv2.COLOR_GRAY2BGR)

    # 将分割掩码叠加到原始图像上
    result = cv2.addWeighted(image, 0.5, mask, 0.5, 0)
    result = cv2.cvtColor(result, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)

    # 保存输出图片
    cv2.imwrite(output_image_path, result)
```

### 代码说明
1. **初始化**：
   - `SAM2ImagePredictor` 类初始化时加载编码器和解码器模型。
   - 使用 `onnxruntime` 加载 ONNX 模型。

2. **设置输入图像**：
   - `set_image` 方法将输入图像传递给编码器模型，获取编码器的特征。

3. **预测**：
   - `predict` 方法根据输入的特征、点提示、边框提示等信息，调用解码器模型进行分割预测

## 回答
根据错误信息和搜索结果，问题的根本原因是输入图像的维度与模型期望的维度不匹配。具体来说，模型期望的输入维度是 `[1, 3, 1024, 1024]`，而实际输入的维度是 `[1, 480, 640, 3]`。

### 解决方案
1. **检查模型的输入维度**：
   使用以下代码检查模型的输入维度，确保输入数据的形状与模型期望的形状一致：

   ```python
   import onnxruntime as ort

   # 加载模型
   session = ort.InferenceSession(encoder_model_path)

   # 打印输入信息
   for input_info in session.get_inputs():
       print(f"Name: {input_info.name}, Shape: {input_info.shape}, Type: {input_info.type}")
   ```

   这将输出模型的输入名称、形状和类型。根据输出信息，调整输入数据的形状。

2. **调整输入图像的维度**：
   根据模型的期望输入维度，调整输入图像的形状。如果模型期望的输入维度是 `[1, 3, 1024, 1024]`，则需要将输入图像调整为该形状。

   修改代码如下：

   ```python
   def set_image(self, image):
       """
       设置输入图像并获取编码器的特征
       :param image: 输入图像
       :return: 编码器的特征
       """
       # 调整图像大小为模型期望的尺寸
       image = cv2.resize(image, (1024, 1024))
       # 调整通道顺序为模型期望的顺序（CHW）
       image = np.transpose(image, (2, 0, 1))
       # 添加批量维度
       image = np.expand_dims(image, axis=0).astype(np.float32)
       outputs = self.encoder_session.run(None, {"image": image})
       high_res_feats_0, high_res_feats_1, image_embed = outputs
       features = {
           "high_res_feats_0": high_res_feats_0,
           "high_res_feats_1": high_res_feats_1,
           "image_embed": image_embed
       }
       return features
   ```

3. **检查模型导出时的输入维度**：
   如果你有原始模型的训练代码，确保在导出模型时指定正确的输入维度。例如，在 PyTorch 中导出 ONNX 模型时，可以指定输入张量的形状：

   ```python
   import torch
   import torch.onnx

   # 加载模型
   model = ...  # 加载你的模型
   model.eval()

   # 创建一个虚拟输入张量，形状与模型期望的输入一致
   dummy_input = torch.randn(1, 3, 1024, 1024)

   # 导出模型到 ONNX 格式
   torch.onnx.export(model, dummy_input, "model.onnx", opset_version=11)
   ```

4. **重新导出模型**：
   如果模型的输入维度与实际需求不一致，可以重新导出模型，确保输入维度正确。例如，调整模型的输入维度为 `[1, 3, 1024, 1024]`，然后重新导出 ONNX 模型。

5. **检查 ONNX Runtime 版本**：
   确保你使用的 ONNX Runtime 版本与模型的版本兼容。如果版本不兼容，可能会导致类似的错误。可以通过以下命令更新 ONNX Runtime：

   ```bash
   pip install --upgrade onnxruntime
   ```

### 总结
错误的根本原因是输入图像的维度与模型期望的维度不匹配。通过检查模型的输入维度、调整输入图像的形状、重新导出模型以及更新 ONNX Runtime 版本，可以解决这个问题。

# 问题

```sh
    masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks = predictor.predict(features, orig_hw)
                                            ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  File "/home/qsbye/Documents/ByeIO/工程文件/exp209-bye_deepseek_mcp_discovery_slam_rs/crates/seekslam_examples/src/segment_onnx.py", line 133, in predict
    raise ValueError("concat_points must be exists")
ValueError: concat_points must be exists
```

## 回答
根据错误信息和搜索结果，问题的根本原因在于 `predict` 方法中缺少必要的输入参数，导致 `concat_points` 为 `None`，从而触发了 `ValueError`。此外，警告信息表明模型的输入维度可能存在问题，具体是 `/image_encoder/trunk/Concat_3_output_0` 的维度不匹配。

### 解决步骤

#### 1. 确保输入参数不为 `None`
在 `predict` 方法中，`point_coords` 和 `box` 都是可选参数，但如果它们都为 `None`，则会导致 `concat_points` 也为 `None`，从而触发错误。需要确保至少有一个提示（点提示或边框提示）被提供，或者修改代码以处理没有提示的情况。

#### 2. 检查输入数据的维度
警告信息表明模型的输入维度可能存在问题。需要确保输入数据的维度与模型期望的维度一致。可以通过打印模型的输入信息来确认这一点。

#### 3. 修改代码以处理没有提示的情况
如果在某些情况下没有点提示或边框提示，可以添加默认值或处理逻辑，以避免 `concat_points` 为 `None`。

### 修改后的代码
以下是修改后的代码，添加了对没有提示的情况的处理，并确保输入数据的维度正确：

```python
def predict(self, features, orig_hw, point_coords=None, point_labels=None, box=None, mask_input=None):
    """
    进行图像分割预测
    :param features: 编码器的特征
    :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
    :param point_coords: 点提示的坐标
    :param point_labels: 点提示的标签
    :param box: 边框提示
    :param mask_input: 掩码输入
    :return: 分割掩码、IoU 预测值和低分辨率掩码
    """
    if point_coords is not None and len(point_coords) != 0:
        point_coords = point_coords.astype(np.float32)
        unnorm_coords = self.transform_coords(point_coords, orig_hw)
        labels = point_labels.astype(np.int64)
        if len(unnorm_coords.shape) == 2:
            unnorm_coords, labels = unnorm_coords[None, ...], labels[None, ...]
    else:
        unnorm_coords, labels = None, None

    if box is not None:
        box = box.astype(np.float32)
        unnorm_box = self.transform_boxes(box, orig_hw)
    else:
        unnorm_box = None

    if mask_input is not None:
        mask_input = mask_input.astype(np.float32)
        if len(mask_input.shape) == 3:
            mask_input = mask_input[None, :, :, :]
    else:
        mask_input = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)

    # 如果没有点提示和边框提示，创建一个默认的点提示
    if unnorm_coords is None and unnorm_box is None:
        unnorm_coords = np.array([[[0.5, 0.5]]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认点提示在图像中心
        labels = np.array([[1]], dtype=np.int64)  # 默认标签为1

    if unnorm_coords is not None:
        concat_points = (unnorm_coords, labels)
    else:
        concat_points = None

    if unnorm_box is not None:
        box_coords = unnorm_box.reshape(-1, 2, 2)
        box_labels = np.array([[2, 3]])
        box_labels = box_labels.repeat(unnorm_box.shape[0], 1)
        if concat_points is not None:
            concat_coords = np.concatenate([box_coords, concat_points[0]], axis=1)
            concat_labels = np.concatenate([box_labels, concat_points[1]], axis=1)
            concat_points = (concat_coords, concat_labels.astype(np.int32))
        else:
            concat_points = (box_coords, box_labels.astype(np.int32))

    if mask_input is None:
        mask_input_dummy = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)
        masks_enable = np.array([0], dtype=np.int32)
    else:
        mask_input_dummy = mask_input
        masks_enable = np.array([1], dtype=np.int32)

    if concat_points is None:
        raise ValueError("concat_points must be exists")

    sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings, dense_pe = self.decoder_session.run(
        None,
        {
            "coords": concat_points[0],
            "labels": concat_points[1],
            "masks": mask_input_dummy,
            "masks_enable": masks_enable,
            "image_embeddings": features["image_embed"],
            "high_res_features1": features["high_res_feats_0"],
            "high_res_features2": features["high_res_feats_1"]
        }
    )

    masks, iou_pred = sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings

    low_res_masks = masks[:, 1:, :, :]
    iou_predictions = iou_pred[:, 1:]

    masks = self.postprocess_masks(masks, orig_hw)

    return masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks
```

### 检查输入数据的维度
在调用模型之前，打印输入数据的维度，确保它们与模型的期望一致：

```python
print("Input image shape:", image.shape)
print("Unnorm coords shape:", unnorm_coords.shape if unnorm_coords is not None else "None")
print("Labels shape:", labels.shape if labels is not None else "None")
print("Unnorm box shape:", unnorm_box.shape if unnorm_box is not None else "None")
print("Mask input shape:", mask_input.shape if mask_input is not None else "None")
```

### 总结
通过确保输入参数不为 `None`，并检查输入数据的维度，可以解决 `ValueError` 和维度不匹配的问题。如果问题仍然存在，可以使用工具如 Netron 来可视化 ONNX 模型，检查每个层的张量形状，以进一步调试问题。

代码:
```py
# 测试SAM2.1图片分割

# 编码模型: ../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.encoder.onnx
## 输出: 
## high_res_feats_0
## name: high_res_feats_0
## tensor: float32[Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_0,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_1,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_2,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_3]
## high_res_feats_1
## name: high_res_feats_1
## tensor: float32[Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_0,Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_1,Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_2,Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_3]
## image_embed
## name: image_embed
## tensor: float32[Reshapeimage_embed_dim_0,Reshapeimage_embed_dim_1,Reshapeimage_embed_dim_2,Reshapeimage_embed_dim_3]

# 解码模型: ../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.decoder.onnx
## 输出:
## masks
## name: masks
## tensor: float32[Resizemasks_dim_0,Resizemasks_dim_1,Resizemasks_dim_2,Resizemasks_dim_3]
## iou_predictions
## name: iou_predictions

# 输出../result/segment_onnx.png

import os
import cv2
import numpy as np
import onnxruntime as ort

class SAM2ImagePredictor:
    def __init__(self, encoder_model_path, decoder_model_path):
        """
        初始化 SAM2 图像分割预测器
        :param encoder_model_path: 编码器模型路径
        :param decoder_model_path: 解码器模型路径
        """
        self.encoder_session = ort.InferenceSession(encoder_model_path)
        self.decoder_session = ort.InferenceSession(decoder_model_path)

    def trunc_normal(self, size, std=0.02, a=-2, b=2):
        """
        生成截断正态分布的随机数
        :param size: 输出数组的形状
        :param std: 标准差
        :param a: 截断下限
        :param b: 截断上限
        :return: 截断正态分布的随机数数组
        """
        values = np.random.normal(loc=0., scale=std, size=size)
        values = np.clip(values, a * std, b * std)
        return values

    def set_image(self, image):
        """
        设置输入图像并获取编码器的特征
        :param image: 输入图像
        :return: 编码器的特征
        """
        # 调整图像大小为模型期望的尺寸
        image = cv2.resize(image, (1024, 1024))
        # 调整通道顺序为模型期望的顺序（CHW）
        image = np.transpose(image, (2, 0, 1))
        # 添加批量维度
        image = np.expand_dims(image, axis=0).astype(np.float32)
        outputs = self.encoder_session.run(None, {"image": image})
        high_res_feats_0, high_res_feats_1, image_embed = outputs
        features = {
            "high_res_feats_0": high_res_feats_0,
            "high_res_feats_1": high_res_feats_1,
            "image_embed": image_embed
        }
        return features

    def predict(self, features, orig_hw, point_coords=None, point_labels=None, box=None, mask_input=None):
        """
        进行图像分割预测
        :param features: 编码器的特征
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :param point_coords: 点提示的坐标
        :param point_labels: 点提示的标签
        :param box: 边框提示
        :param mask_input: 掩码输入
        :return: 分割掩码、IoU 预测值和低分辨率掩码
        """
        if point_coords is not None and len(point_coords) != 0:
            point_coords = point_coords.astype(np.float32)
            unnorm_coords = self.transform_coords(point_coords, orig_hw)
            labels = point_labels.astype(np.int64)
            if len(unnorm_coords.shape) == 2:
                unnorm_coords, labels = unnorm_coords[None, ...], labels[None, ...]
        else:
            unnorm_coords, labels = None, None

        if box is not None:
            box = box.astype(np.float32)
            unnorm_box = self.transform_boxes(box, orig_hw)
        else:
            unnorm_box = None

        if mask_input is not None:
            mask_input = mask_input.astype(np.float32)
            if len(mask_input.shape) == 3:
                mask_input = mask_input[None, :, :, :]
        else:
            mask_input = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)

        # 如果没有点提示和边框提示，创建一个默认的点提示
        if unnorm_coords is None and unnorm_box is None:
            unnorm_coords = np.array([[[0.5, 0.5]]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认点提示在图像中心
            labels = np.array([[1]], dtype=np.int64)  # 默认标签为1

        if unnorm_coords is not None:
            concat_points = (unnorm_coords, labels)
        else:
            concat_points = None

        if unnorm_box is not None:
            box_coords = unnorm_box.reshape(-1, 2, 2)
            box_labels = np.array([[2, 3]])
            box_labels = box_labels.repeat(unnorm_box.shape[0], 1)
            if concat_points is not None:
                concat_coords = np.concatenate([box_coords, concat_points[0]], axis=1)
                concat_labels = np.concatenate([box_labels, concat_points[1]], axis=1)
                concat_points = (concat_coords, concat_labels.astype(np.int32))
            else:
                concat_points = (box_coords, box_labels.astype(np.int32))

        if mask_input is None:
            mask_input_dummy = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)
            masks_enable = np.array([0], dtype=np.int32)
        else:
            mask_input_dummy = mask_input
            masks_enable = np.array([1], dtype=np.int32)

        if concat_points is None:
            raise ValueError("concat_points must be exists")

        sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings, dense_pe = self.decoder_session.run(
            None,
            {
                "coords": concat_points[0],
                "labels": concat_points[1],
                "masks": mask_input_dummy,
                "masks_enable": masks_enable,
                "image_embeddings": features["image_embed"],
                "high_res_features1": features["high_res_feats_0"],
                "high_res_features2": features["high_res_feats_1"]
            }
        )

        masks, iou_pred = sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings

        low_res_masks = masks[:, 1:, :, :]
        iou_predictions = iou_pred[:, 1:]

        masks = self.postprocess_masks(masks, orig_hw)

        return masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks

    def transform_coords(self, coords, orig_hw):
        """
        转换坐标
        :param coords: 坐标
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 转换后的坐标
        """
        h, w = orig_hw
        coords = coords.copy()
        coords[..., 0] = coords[..., 0] / w
        coords[..., 1] = coords[..., 1] / h

        resolution = 1024
        coords = coords * resolution
        return coords

    def transform_boxes(self, boxes, orig_hw):
        """
        转换边框
        :param boxes: 边框
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 转换后的边框
        """
        boxes = self.transform_coords(boxes.reshape(-1, 2, 2), orig_hw)
        return boxes

    def postprocess_masks(self, masks, orig_hw):
        """
        后处理分割掩码
        :param masks: 分割掩码
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 后处理后的分割掩码
        """
        interpolated_masks = []
        for mask in masks:
            mask = np.transpose(mask, (1, 2, 0))
            resized_mask = cv2.resize(mask, (orig_hw[1], orig_hw[0]), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
            resized_mask = np.transpose(resized_mask, (2, 0, 1))
            interpolated_masks.append(resized_mask)
        interpolated_masks = np.array(interpolated_masks)

        return interpolated_masks


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 输入图片路径
    input_image_path = "../assets/rgb1.png"
    # 输出图片路径
    output_image_path = "../result/segment_onnx.png"
    # 编码器模型路径
    encoder_model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.encoder.onnx"
    # 解码器模型路径
    decoder_model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.decoder.onnx"

    # 读取输入图片
    image = cv2.imread(input_image_path)
    image = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
    orig_hw = image.shape[:2]

    # 初始化 SAM2 图像分割预测器
    predictor = SAM2ImagePredictor(encoder_model_path, decoder_model_path)

    # 设置输入图像并获取编码器的特征
    features = predictor.set_image(image)

    # 进行图像分割预测
    masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks = predictor.predict(features, orig_hw)

    # 将分割掩码转换为可视化图像
    mask = masks[0, 0].astype(np.uint8) * 255
    mask = cv2.cvtColor(mask, cv2.COLOR_GRAY2BGR)

    # 将分割掩码叠加到原始图像上
    result = cv2.addWeighted(image, 0.5, mask, 0.5, 0)
    result = cv2.cvtColor(result, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)

    # 保存输出图片
    cv2.imwrite(output_image_path, result)
```

## 回答
从报错信息来看，问题主要出现在调用 `decoder_session.run()` 时，输入的参数名称与模型期望的输入名称不匹配。具体来说，模型期望的输入名称是 `['image_embed', 'high_res_feats_0', 'high_res_feats_1', 'point_coords', 'point_labels', 'mask_input', 'has_mask_input', 'orig_im_size']`，但实际传入的输入名称是 `['coords', 'labels', 'masks', 'masks_enable', 'image_embeddings', 'high_res_features1', 'high_res_features2']`。

以下是解决这个问题的步骤：

### 1. 检查模型的输入名称
首先，需要确认 `decoder_model_path` 指向的模型文件的输入名称。可以使用 `onnxruntime` 的 `get_inputs()` 方法来获取模型的输入信息。

在代码中添加以下调试代码，打印出解码器模型的输入信息：
```py
for input_info in predictor.decoder_session.get_inputs():
    print(f"Input name: {input_info.name}, shape: {input_info.shape}, type: {input_info.type}")
```

运行代码后，观察输出的输入名称，确保它们与代码中使用的输入名称一致。

### 2. 修改输入参数名称
根据模型的实际输入名称，修改代码中调用 `decoder_session.run()` 时的输入参数名称。例如，如果模型期望的输入名称是 `image_embed`，而代码中使用的是 `image_embeddings`，则需要将代码中的 `image_embeddings` 改为 `image_embed`。

以下是修改后的代码片段：
```py
sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings, dense_pe = self.decoder_session.run(
    None,
    {
        "point_coords": concat_points[0],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
        "point_labels": concat_points[1],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
        "mask_input": mask_input_dummy,   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
        "has_mask_input": masks_enable,   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
        "orig_im_size": np.array(orig_hw, dtype=np.float32),  # 添加模型期望的输入
        "image_embed": features["image_embed"],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
        "high_res_feats_0": features["high_res_feats_0"],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
        "high_res_feats_1": features["high_res_feats_1"]   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
    }
)
```

### 3. 添加缺失的输入参数
从报错信息中可以看到，模型还期望一个名为 `orig_im_size` 的输入参数，但代码中没有提供。根据模型的输入要求，`orig_im_size` 应该是一个包含原始图像高和宽的数组。在代码中添加这个参数：
```py
orig_im_size = np.array(orig_hw, dtype=np.float32)
```
然后在调用 `decoder_session.run()` 时传入该参数。

### 4. 确保输入数据的形状和类型正确
除了名称外，还需要确保输入数据的形状和类型与模型的要求一致。可以通过打印模型的输入信息来确认这一点。例如：
```py
for input_info in predictor.decoder_session.get_inputs():
    print(f"Input name: {input_info.name}, shape: {input_info.shape}, type: {input_info.type}")
```

如果发现输入数据的形状或类型不匹配，需要根据模型的要求进行调整。例如，如果模型期望 `point_coords` 的形状是 `(1, N, 2)`，而代码中传入的是 `(N, 2)`，则需要在代码中添加必要的维度：
```py
if len(unnorm_coords.shape) == 2:
    unnorm_coords = unnorm_coords[None, ...]
```

### 5. 完整的修改后的代码
以下是修改后的完整代码片段：
```py
def predict(self, features, orig_hw, point_coords=None, point_labels=None, box=None, mask_input=None):
    """
    进行图像分割预测
    :param features: 编码器的特征
    :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
    :param point_coords: 点提示的坐标
    :param point_labels: 点提示的标签
    :param box: 边框提示
    :param mask_input: 掩码输入
    :return: 分割掩码、IoU 预测值和低分辨率掩码
    """
    if point_coords is not None and len(point_coords) != 0:
        point_coords = point_coords.astype(np.float32)
        unnorm_coords = self.transform_coords(point_coords, orig_hw)
        labels = point_labels.astype(np.int64)
        if len(unnorm_coords.shape) == 2:
            unnorm_coords, labels = unnorm_coords[None, ...], labels[None, ...]
    else:
        unnorm_coords, labels = None, None

    if box is not None:
        box = box.astype(np.float32)
        unnorm_box = self.transform_boxes(box, orig_hw)
    else:
        unnorm_box = None

    if mask_input is not None:
        mask_input = mask_input.astype(np.float32)
        if len(mask_input.shape) == 3:
            mask_input = mask_input[None, :, :, :]
    else:
        mask_input = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)

    # 如果没有点提示和边框提示，创建一个默认的点提示
    if unnorm_coords is None and unnorm_box is None:
        unnorm_coords = np.array([[[0.5, 0.5]]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认点提示在图像中心
        labels = np.array([[1]], dtype=np.int64)  # 默认标签为1

    if unnorm_coords is not None:
        concat_points = (unnorm_coords, labels)
    else:
        concat_points = None

    if unnorm_box is not None:
        box_coords = unnorm_box.reshape(-1, 2, 2)
        box_labels = np.array([[2, 3]])
        box_labels = box_labels.repeat(unnorm_box.shape[0], 1)
        if concat_points is not None:
            concat_coords = np.concatenate([box_coords, concat_points[0]], axis=1)
            concat_labels = np.concatenate([box_labels, concat_points[1]], axis=1)
            concat_points = (concat_coords, concat_labels.astype(np.int32))
        else:
            concat_points = (box_coords, box_labels.astype(np.int32))

    if mask_input is None:
        mask_input_dummy = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)
        masks_enable = np.array([0], dtype=np.int32)
    else:
        mask_input_dummy = mask_input
        masks_enable = np.array([1], dtype=np.int32)

    if concat_points is None:
        raise ValueError("concat_points must be exists")

    orig_im_size = np.array(orig_hw, dtype=np.float32)

    sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings, dense_pe = self.decoder_session.run(
        None,
        {
            "point_coords": concat_points[0],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
            "point_labels": concat_points[1],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
            "mask_input": mask_input_dummy,   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
            "has_mask_input": masks_enable,   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
            "orig_im_size": orig_im_size,     # 添加模型期望的输入
            "image_embed": features["image_embed"],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
            "high_res_feats_0": features["high_res_feats_0"],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
            "high_res_feats_1": features["high_res_feats_1"]   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
        }
    )

    masks, iou_pred = sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings

    low_res_masks = masks[:, 1:, :, :]
    iou_predictions = iou_pred[:, 1:]

    masks = self.postprocess_masks(masks, orig_hw)

    return masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks
```

### 6. 测试代码
完成上述修改后，重新运行代码，观察是否还有报错。如果问题解决，代码应该能够正常运行并生成分割结果。

如果仍有问题，请检查模型文件是否正确，以及输入数据是否符合模型的要求。

## 回答
这个新的错误提示表明，模型期望某些输入的类型是 `float`，但实际传入的类型是 `int64`。具体来说，错误信息指出 `point_labels` 的类型应该是 `float`，而不是 `int64`。

### 解决方法
我们需要将 `point_labels` 的类型从 `int64` 转换为 `float`。以下是修改后的代码片段：

#### 修改 `predict` 函数
在 `predict` 函数中，将 `point_labels` 和 `box_labels` 的类型从 `int64` 转换为 `float32`：

```py
def predict(self, features, orig_hw, point_coords=None, point_labels=None, box=None, mask_input=None):
    """
    进行图像分割预测
    :param features: 编码器的特征
    :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
    :param point_coords: 点提示的坐标
    :param point_labels: 点提示的标签
    :param box: 边框提示
    :param mask_input: 掩码输入
    :return: 分割掩码、IoU 预测值和低分辨率掩码
    """
    if point_coords is not None and len(point_coords) != 0:
        point_coords = point_coords.astype(np.float32)
        unnorm_coords = self.transform_coords(point_coords, orig_hw)
        labels = point_labels.astype(np.float32)  # 修改为 float32
        if len(unnorm_coords.shape) == 2:
            unnorm_coords, labels = unnorm_coords[None, ...], labels[None, ...]
    else:
        unnorm_coords, labels = None, None

    if box is not None:
        box = box.astype(np.float32)
        unnorm_box = self.transform_boxes(box, orig_hw)
    else:
        unnorm_box = None

    if mask_input is not None:
        mask_input = mask_input.astype(np.float32)
        if len(mask_input.shape) == 3:
            mask_input = mask_input[None, :, :, :]
    else:
        mask_input = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)

    # 如果没有点提示和边框提示，创建一个默认的点提示
    if unnorm_coords is None and unnorm_box is None:
        unnorm_coords = np.array([[[0.5, 0.5]]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认点提示在图像中心
        labels = np.array([[1]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认标签为1，修改为 float32

    if unnorm_coords is not None:
        concat_points = (unnorm_coords, labels)
    else:
        concat_points = None

    if unnorm_box is not None:
        box_coords = unnorm_box.reshape(-1, 2, 2)
        box_labels = np.array([[2, 3]], dtype=np.float32)  # 修改为 float32
        box_labels = box_labels.repeat(unnorm_box.shape[0], 1)
        if concat_points is not None:
            concat_coords = np.concatenate([box_coords, concat_points[0]], axis=1)
            concat_labels = np.concatenate([box_labels, concat_points[1]], axis=1)
            concat_points = (concat_coords, concat_labels)
        else:
            concat_points = (box_coords, box_labels)

    if mask_input is None:
        mask_input_dummy = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)
        masks_enable = np.array([0], dtype=np.int32)
    else:
        mask_input_dummy = mask_input
        masks_enable = np.array([1], dtype=np.int32)

    if concat_points is None:
        raise ValueError("concat_points must be exists")

    orig_im_size = np.array(orig_hw, dtype=np.float32)

    sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings, dense_pe = self.decoder_session.run(
        None,
        {
            "point_coords": concat_points[0],
            "point_labels": concat_points[1],
            "mask_input": mask_input_dummy,
            "has_mask_input": masks_enable,
            "orig_im_size": orig_im_size,
            "image_embed": features["image_embed"],
            "high_res_feats_0": features["high_res_feats_0"],
            "high_res_feats_1": features["high_res_feats_1"]
        }
    )

    masks, iou_pred = sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings

    low_res_masks = masks[:, 1:, :, :]
    iou_predictions = iou_pred[:, 1:]

    masks = self.postprocess_masks(masks, orig_hw)

    return masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks
```

### 说明
1. **`point_labels` 和 `box_labels` 的类型**：将它们从 `int64` 转换为 `float32`。
2. **其他输入的类型**：确保所有输入的类型都符合模型的要求。如果还有其他输入类型不匹配，可以类似地进行转换。

### 测试代码
完成上述修改后，重新运行代码，观察是否还有报错。如果问题解决，代码应该能够正常运行并生成分割结果。

如果仍有问题，请检查模型文件是否正确，以及输入数据是否符合模型的要求。

# 问题
```py
# 测试SAM2.1图片分割

# 编码模型: ../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.encoder.onnx
## 输出: 
## high_res_feats_0
## name: high_res_feats_0
## tensor: float32[Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_0,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_1,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_2,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_3]
## high_res_feats_1
## name: high_res_feats_1
## tensor: float32[Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_0,Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_1,Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_2,Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_3]
## image_embed
## name: image_embed
## tensor: float32[Reshapeimage_embed_dim_0,Reshapeimage_embed_dim_1,Reshapeimage_embed_dim_2,Reshapeimage_embed_dim_3]

# 解码模型: ../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.decoder.onnx
## 输出:
## masks
## name: masks
## tensor: float32[Resizemasks_dim_0,Resizemasks_dim_1,Resizemasks_dim_2,Resizemasks_dim_3]
## iou_predictions
## name: iou_predictions
## tensor: float32[Resizemasks_dim_0,Whereiou_predictions_dim_1]

# 输入图片../assets/rgb1.png
# 输出../result/segment_onnx.png

import os
import cv2
import numpy as np
import onnxruntime as ort

class SAM2ImagePredictor:
    def __init__(self, encoder_model_path, decoder_model_path):
        """
        初始化 SAM2 图像分割预测器
        :param encoder_model_path: 编码器模型路径
        :param decoder_model_path: 解码器模型路径
        """
        self.encoder_session = ort.InferenceSession(encoder_model_path)
        self.decoder_session = ort.InferenceSession(decoder_model_path)

    def trunc_normal(self, size, std=0.02, a=-2, b=2):
        """
        生成截断正态分布的随机数
        :param size: 输出数组的形状
        :param std: 标准差
        :param a: 截断下限
        :param b: 截断上限
        :return: 截断正态分布的随机数数组
        """
        values = np.random.normal(loc=0., scale=std, size=size)
        values = np.clip(values, a * std, b * std)
        return values

    def set_image(self, image):
        """
        设置输入图像并获取编码器的特征
        :param image: 输入图像
        :return: 编码器的特征
        """
        # 调整图像大小为模型期望的尺寸
        image = cv2.resize(image, (1024, 1024))
        # 调整通道顺序为模型期望的顺序（CHW）
        image = np.transpose(image, (2, 0, 1))
        # 添加批量维度
        image = np.expand_dims(image, axis=0).astype(np.float32)
        outputs = self.encoder_session.run(None, {"image": image})
        high_res_feats_0, high_res_feats_1, image_embed = outputs
        features = {
            "high_res_feats_0": high_res_feats_0,
            "high_res_feats_1": high_res_feats_1,
            "image_embed": image_embed
        }
        return features

    def predict(self, features, orig_hw, point_coords=None, point_labels=None, box=None, mask_input=None):
        """
        进行图像分割预测
        :param features: 编码器的特征
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :param point_coords: 点提示的坐标
        :param point_labels: 点提示的标签
        :param box: 边框提示
        :param mask_input: 掩码输入
        :return: 分割掩码、IoU 预测值和低分辨率掩码
        """
        if point_coords is not None and len(point_coords) != 0:
            point_coords = point_coords.astype(np.float32)
            unnorm_coords = self.transform_coords(point_coords, orig_hw)
            labels = point_labels.astype(np.float32)
            if len(unnorm_coords.shape) == 2:
                unnorm_coords, labels = unnorm_coords[None, ...], labels[None, ...]
        else:
            unnorm_coords, labels = None, None

        if box is not None:
            box = box.astype(np.float32)
            unnorm_box = self.transform_boxes(box, orig_hw)
        else:
            unnorm_box = None

        if mask_input is not None:
            mask_input = mask_input.astype(np.float32)
            if len(mask_input.shape) == 3:
                mask_input = mask_input[None, :, :, :]
        else:
            mask_input = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)

        # 如果没有点提示和边框提示，创建一个默认的点提示
        if unnorm_coords is None and unnorm_box is None:
            unnorm_coords = np.array([[[0.5, 0.5]]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认点提示在图像中心
            labels = np.array([[1]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认标签为1，修改为 float32

        if unnorm_coords is not None:
            concat_points = (unnorm_coords, labels)
        else:
            concat_points = None

        if unnorm_box is not None:
            box_coords = unnorm_box.reshape(-1, 2, 2)
            box_labels = np.array([[2, 3]], dtype=np.float32)  # 修改为 float32
            box_labels = box_labels.repeat(unnorm_box.shape[0], 1)
            if concat_points is not None:
                concat_coords = np.concatenate([box_coords, concat_points[0]], axis=1)
                concat_labels = np.concatenate([box_labels, concat_points[1]], axis=1)
                concat_points = (concat_coords, concat_labels.astype(np.int32))
            else:
                concat_points = (box_coords, box_labels.astype(np.int32))

        if mask_input is None:
            mask_input_dummy = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)
            masks_enable = np.array([0], dtype=np.int32)
        else:
            mask_input_dummy = mask_input
            masks_enable = np.array([1], dtype=np.int32)

        if concat_points is None:
            raise ValueError("concat_points must be exists")

        orig_im_size = np.array(orig_hw, dtype=np.int32)

        sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings, dense_pe = self.decoder_session.run(
            None,
            {
                "point_coords": concat_points[0],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
                "point_labels": concat_points[1],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
                "mask_input": mask_input_dummy,   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
                "has_mask_input": masks_enable,   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
                "orig_im_size": orig_im_size,     # 添加模型期望的输入
                "image_embed": features["image_embed"],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
                "high_res_feats_0": features["high_res_feats_0"],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
                "high_res_feats_1": features["high_res_feats_1"]   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
            }
        )

        masks, iou_pred = sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings

        low_res_masks = masks[:, 1:, :, :]
        iou_predictions = iou_pred[:, 1:]

        masks = self.postprocess_masks(masks, orig_hw)

        return masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks

    def transform_coords(self, coords, orig_hw):
        """
        转换坐标
        :param coords: 坐标
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 转换后的坐标
        """
        h, w = orig_hw
        coords = coords.copy()
        coords[..., 0] = coords[..., 0] / w
        coords[..., 1] = coords[..., 1] / h

        resolution = 1024
        coords = coords * resolution
        return coords

    def transform_boxes(self, boxes, orig_hw):
        """
        转换边框
        :param boxes: 边框
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 转换后的边框
        """
        boxes = self.transform_coords(boxes.reshape(-1, 2, 2), orig_hw)
        return boxes

    def postprocess_masks(self, masks, orig_hw):
        """
        后处理分割掩码
        :param masks: 分割掩码
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 后处理后的分割掩码
        """
        interpolated_masks = []
        for mask in masks:
            mask = np.transpose(mask, (1, 2, 0))
            resized_mask = cv2.resize(mask, (orig_hw[1], orig_hw[0]), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
            resized_mask = np.transpose(resized_mask, (2, 0, 1))
            interpolated_masks.append(resized_mask)
        interpolated_masks = np.array(interpolated_masks)

        return interpolated_masks


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 输入图片路径
    input_image_path = "../assets/rgb1.png"
    # 输出图片路径
    output_image_path = "../result/segment_onnx.png"
    # 编码器模型路径
    encoder_model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.encoder.onnx"
    # 解码器模型路径
    decoder_model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.decoder.onnx"

    # 读取输入图片
    image = cv2.imread(input_image_path)
    image = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
    orig_hw = image.shape[:2]

    # 初始化 SAM2 图像分割预测器
    predictor = SAM2ImagePredictor(encoder_model_path, decoder_model_path)

    # 设置输入图像并获取编码器的特征
    features = predictor.set_image(image)

    # 进行图像分割预测
    masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks = predictor.predict(features, orig_hw)

    # 将分割掩码转换为可视化图像
    mask = masks[0, 0].astype(np.uint8) * 255
    mask = cv2.cvtColor(mask, cv2.COLOR_GRAY2BGR)

    # 将分割掩码叠加到原始图像上
    result = cv2.addWeighted(image, 0.5, mask, 0.5, 0)
    result = cv2.cvtColor(result, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)

    # 保存输出图片
    cv2.imwrite(output_image_path, result)
```

## 回答
根据报错信息，问题出在 `mask_input` 的维度上。模型期望 `mask_input` 的维度为 4，但代码中提供的 `mask_input` 的维度为 3。我们需要对 `mask_input` 进行调整，确保其维度符合模型的期望。

以下是修正后的代码：

```py
class SAM2ImagePredictor:
    def __init__(self, encoder_model_path, decoder_model_path):
        """
        初始化 SAM2 图像分割预测器
        :param encoder_model_path: 编码器模型路径
        :param decoder_model_path: 解码器模型路径
        """
        self.encoder_session = ort.InferenceSession(encoder_model_path)
        self.decoder_session = ort.InferenceSession(decoder_model_path)

    def trunc_normal(self, size, std=0.02, a=-2, b=2):
        """
        生成截断正态分布的随机数
        :param size: 输出数组的形状
        :param std: 标准差
        :param a: 截断下限
        :param b: 截断上限
        :return: 截断正态分布的随机数数组
        """
        values = np.random.normal(loc=0., scale=std, size=size)
        values = np.clip(values, a * std, b * std)
        return values

    def set_image(self, image):
        """
        设置输入图像并获取编码器的特征
        :param image: 输入图像
        :return: 编码器的特征
        """
        # 调整图像大小为模型期望的尺寸
        image = cv2.resize(image, (1024, 1024))
        # 调整通道顺序为模型期望的顺序（CHW）
        image = np.transpose(image, (2, 0, 1))
        # 添加批量维度
        image = np.expand_dims(image, axis=0).astype(np.float32)
        outputs = self.encoder_session.run(None, {"image": image})
        high_res_feats_0, high_res_feats_1, image_embed = outputs
        features = {
            "high_res_feats_0": high_res_feats_0,
            "high_res_feats_1": high_res_feats_1,
            "image_embed": image_embed
        }
        return features

    def predict(self, features, orig_hw, point_coords=None, point_labels=None, box=None, mask_input=None):
        """
        进行图像分割预测
        :param features: 编码器的特征
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :param point_coords: 点提示的坐标
        :param point_labels: 点提示的标签
        :param box: 边框提示
        :param mask_input: 掩码输入
        :return: 分割掩码、IoU 预测值和低分辨率掩码
        """
        if point_coords is not None and len(point_coords) != 0:
            point_coords = point_coords.astype(np.float32)
            unnorm_coords = self.transform_coords(point_coords, orig_hw)
            labels = point_labels.astype(np.float32)
            if len(unnorm_coords.shape) == 2:
                unnorm_coords, labels = unnorm_coords[None, ...], labels[None, ...]
        else:
            unnorm_coords, labels = None, None

        if box is not None:
            box = box.astype(np.float32)
            unnorm_box = self.transform_boxes(box, orig_hw)
        else:
            unnorm_box = None

        if mask_input is not None:
            mask_input = mask_input.astype(np.float32)
            if len(mask_input.shape) == 3:
                mask_input = mask_input[None, :, :, :]
        else:
            # 确保 mask_input 的维度为 4
            mask_input = np.zeros((1, 1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)

        # 如果没有点提示和边框提示，创建一个默认的点提示
        if unnorm_coords is None and unnorm_box is None:
            unnorm_coords = np.array([[[0.5, 0.5]]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认点提示在图像中心
            labels = np.array([[1]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认标签为1，修改为 float32

        if unnorm_coords is not None:
            concat_points = (unnorm_coords, labels)
        else:
            concat_points = None

        if unnorm_box is not None:
            box_coords = unnorm_box.reshape(-1, 2, 2)
            box_labels = np.array([[2, 3]], dtype=np.float32)  # 修改为 float32
            box_labels = box_labels.repeat(unnorm_box.shape[0], 1)
            if concat_points is not None:
                concat_coords = np.concatenate([box_coords, concat_points[0]], axis=1)
                concat_labels = np.concatenate([box_labels, concat_points[1]], axis=1)
                concat_points = (concat_coords, concat_labels.astype(np.int32))
            else:
                concat_points = (box_coords, box_labels.astype(np.int32))

        if mask_input is None:
            mask_input_dummy = np.zeros((1, 1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)
            masks_enable = np.array([0], dtype=np.int32)
        else:
            mask_input_dummy = mask_input
            masks_enable = np.array([1], dtype=np.int32)

        if concat_points is None:
            raise ValueError("concat_points must be exists")

        orig_im_size = np.array(orig_hw, dtype=np.int32)

        sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings, dense_pe = self.decoder_session.run(
            None,
            {
                "point_coords": concat_points[0],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
                "point_labels": concat_points[1],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
                "mask_input": mask_input_dummy,   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
                "has_mask_input": masks_enable,   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
                "orig_im_size": orig_im_size,     # 添加模型期望的输入
                "image_embed": features["image_embed"],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
                "high_res_feats_0": features["high_res_feats_0"],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
                "high_res_feats_1": features["high_res_feats_1"]   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称
            }
        )

        masks, iou_pred = sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings

        low_res_masks = masks[:, 1:, :, :]
        iou_predictions = iou_pred[:, 1:]

        masks = self.postprocess_masks(masks, orig_hw)

        return masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks

    def transform_coords(self, coords, orig_hw):
        """
        转换坐标
        :param coords: 坐标
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 转换后的坐标
        """
        h, w = orig_hw
        coords = coords.copy()
        coords[..., 0] = coords[..., 0] / w
        coords[..., 1] = coords[..., 1] / h

        resolution = 1024
        coords = coords * resolution
        return coords

    def transform_boxes(self, boxes, orig_hw):
        """
        转换边框
        :param boxes: 边框
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 转换后的边框
        """
        boxes = self.transform_coords(boxes.reshape(-1, 2, 2), orig_hw)
        return boxes

    def postprocess_masks(self, masks, orig_hw):
        """
        后处理分割掩码
        :param masks: 分割掩码
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 后处理后的分割掩码
        """
        interpolated_masks = []
        for mask in masks:
            mask = np.transpose(mask, (1, 2, 0))
            resized_mask = cv2.resize(mask, (orig_hw[1], orig_hw[0]), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
            resized_mask = np.transpose(resized_mask, (2, 0, 1))
            interpolated_masks.append(resized_mask)
        interpolated_masks = np.array(interpolated_masks)

        return interpolated_masks


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 输入图片路径
    input_image_path = "../assets/rgb1.png"
    # 输出图片路径
    output_image_path = "../result/segment_onnx.png"
    # 编码器模型路径
    encoder_model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.encoder.onnx"
    # 解码器模型路径
    decoder_model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.decoder.onnx"

    # 读取输入图片
    image = cv2.imread(input)
```

## 回答
根据报错信息，问题出在 postprocess_masks 函数中，np.transpose 的调用导致了错误。具体来说，resized_mask 的维度与 np.transpose 的参数 (2, 0, 1) 不匹配。
问题分析
在 postprocess_masks 函数中，masks 是一个 4 维数组，形状为 (batch_size, num_masks, height, width)。在对每个 mask 进行 np.transpose 和 cv2.resize 操作时，需要确保 mask 的形状与操作一致。
解决方法
需要确保 mask 的形状在 np.transpose 和 cv2.resize 操作之前是正确的。以下是修正后的代码：
Python
```py
def postprocess_masks(self, masks, orig_hw):
    """
    后处理分割掩码
    :param masks: 分割掩码
    :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
    :return: 后处理后的分割掩码
    """
    interpolated_masks = []
    for mask in masks:
        # 确保 mask 的形状为 (num_masks, height, width)
        mask = np.squeeze(mask, axis=0)  # 去掉 batch 维度
        mask = np.transpose(mask, (1, 2, 0))  # 转换为 (height, width, num_masks)
        resized_mask = cv2.resize(mask, (orig_hw[1], orig_hw[0]), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
        resized_mask = np.transpose(resized_mask, (2, 0, 1))  # 转换回 (num_masks, height, width)
        interpolated_masks.append(resized_mask)
    interpolated_masks = np.array(interpolated_masks)

    return interpolated_masks
```

修正代码;

## 回答
从错误信息来看，问题出在 `np.transpose(mask, (1, 2, 0))` 这一行，原因是 `mask` 的维度不符合预期，导致无法按照 `(1, 2, 0)` 的顺序进行转置。

我们需要先检查 `mask` 的形状，确保其维度和顺序符合预期。以下是修正后的代码：

```py
def postprocess_masks(self, masks, orig_hw):
    """
    后处理分割掩码
    :param masks: 分割掩码
    :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
    :return: 后处理后的分割掩码
    """
    interpolated_masks = []
    for mask in masks:
        # 打印 mask 的形状，便于调试
        print("Original mask shape:", mask.shape)

        # 确保 mask 的形状为 (num_masks, height, width)
        if len(mask.shape) == 4:  # 如果有 batch 维度，去掉 batch 维度
            mask = np.squeeze(mask, axis=0)
        if len(mask.shape) == 3:  # 如果已经是 (num_masks, height, width)，直接使用
            pass
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unexpected mask shape: {mask.shape}")

        # 转换为 (height, width, num_masks)
        mask = np.transpose(mask, (1, 2, 0))

        # 调整大小到原始图像尺寸
        resized_mask = cv2.resize(mask, (orig_hw[1], orig_hw[0]), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)

        # 转换回 (num_masks, height, width)
        resized_mask = np.transpose(resized_mask, (2, 0, 1))

        interpolated_masks.append(resized_mask)

    interpolated_masks = np.array(interpolated_masks)

    return interpolated_masks
```

### 修正点说明
1. **检查 `mask` 的维度**：
   - 使用 `print("Original mask shape:", mask.shape)` 打印 `mask` 的形状，便于调试。
   - 如果 `mask` 的形状为 `(1, num_masks, height, width)`，则去掉 batch 维度。
   - 如果 `mask` 的形状已经是 `(num_masks, height, width)`，则直接使用。
   - 如果 `mask` 的形状不符合预期，抛出错误提示。

2. **确保转置操作的合法性**：
   - 在进行 `np.transpose(mask, (1, 2, 0))` 之前，确保 `mask` 的形状为 `(num_masks, height, width)`，这样转置操作才合法。

3. **调整大小和转置**：
   - 使用 `cv2.resize` 调整掩码大小到原始图像的尺寸。
   - 将调整后的掩码转置回 `(num_masks, height, width)` 的形状。

### 调试建议
运行修正后的代码时，观察打印的 `mask` 形状，确保其符合预期。如果仍有问题，可以根据打印的形状进一步调整代码逻辑。

# 问题
```py
# 测试SAM2.1图片分割

# 编码模型: ../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.encoder.onnx
## 输出: 
## high_res_feats_0
## name: high_res_feats_0
## tensor: float32[Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_0,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_1,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_2,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_3]
## high_res_feats_1
## name: high_res_feats_1
## tensor: float32[Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_0,Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_1,Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_2,Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_3]
## image_embed
## name: image_embed
## tensor: float32[Reshapeimage_embed_dim_0,Reshapeimage_embed_dim_1,Reshapeimage_embed_dim_2,Reshapeimage_embed_dim_3]

# 解码模型: ../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.decoder.onnx
## 输出:
## masks
## name: masks
## tensor: float32[Resizemasks_dim_0,Resizemasks_dim_1,Resizemasks_dim_2,Resizemasks_dim_3]
## iou_predictions
## name: iou_predictions
## tensor: float32[Resizemasks_dim_0,Whereiou_predictions_dim_1]

# 输入图片../assets/rgb1.png
# 输出../result/segment_onnx.png

import os
import cv2
import numpy as np
import onnxruntime as ort

class SAM2ImagePredictor:
    def __init__(self, encoder_model_path, decoder_model_path):
        """
        初始化 SAM2 图像分割预测器
        :param encoder_model_path: 编码器模型路径
        :param decoder_model_path: 解码器模型路径
        """
        self.encoder_session = ort.InferenceSession(encoder_model_path)
        self.decoder_session = ort.InferenceSession(decoder_model_path)

    def trunc_normal(self, size, std=0.02, a=-2, b=2):
        """
        生成截断正态分布的随机数
        :param size: 输出数组的形状
        :param std: 标准差
        :param a: 截断下限
        :param b: 截断上限
        :return: 截断正态分布的随机数数组
        """
        values = np.random.normal(loc=0., scale=std, size=size)
        values = np.clip(values, a * std, b * std)
        return values

    def set_image(self, image):
        """
        设置输入图像并获取编码器的特征
        :param image: 输入图像
        :return: 编码器的特征
        """
        # 调整图像大小为模型期望的尺寸
        image = cv2.resize(image, (1024, 1024))
        # 调整通道顺序为模型期望的顺序（CHW）
        image = np.transpose(image, (2, 0, 1))
        # 添加批量维度
        image = np.expand_dims(image, axis=0).astype(np.float32)
        outputs = self.encoder_session.run(None, {"image": image})
        high_res_feats_0, high_res_feats_1, image_embed = outputs
        features = {
            "high_res_feats_0": high_res_feats_0,
            "high_res_feats_1": high_res_feats_1,
            "image_embed": image_embed
        }
        return features

    def predict(self, features, orig_hw, point_coords=None, point_labels=None, box=None, mask_input=None):
        """
        进行图像分割预测
        :param features: 编码器的特征
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :param point_coords: 点提示的坐标
        :param point_labels: 点提示的标签
        :param box: 边框提示
        :param mask_input: 掩码输入
        :return: 分割掩码、IoU 预测值和低分辨率掩码
        """
        if point_coords is not None and len(point_coords) != 0:
            point_coords = point_coords.astype(np.float32)
            unnorm_coords = self.transform_coords(point_coords, orig_hw)
            labels = point_labels.astype(np.float32)
            if len(unnorm_coords.shape) == 2:
                unnorm_coords, labels = unnorm_coords[None, ...], labels[None, ...]
        else:
            unnorm_coords, labels = None, None

        if box is not None:
            box = box.astype(np.float32)
            unnorm_box = self.transform_boxes(box, orig_hw)
        else:
            unnorm_box = None

        if mask_input is not None:
            mask_input = mask_input.astype(np.float32)
            if len(mask_input.shape) == 3:
                mask_input = mask_input[None, :, :, :]
        else:
            # 确保 mask_input 的维度为 4
            mask_input = np.zeros((1, 1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)

        # 如果没有点提示和边框提示，创建一个默认的点提示
        if unnorm_coords is None and unnorm_box is None:
            unnorm_coords = np.array([[[0.5, 0.5]]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认点提示在图像中心
            labels = np.array([[1]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认标签为1，修改为 float32

        if unnorm_coords is not None:
            concat_points = (unnorm_coords, labels)
        else:
            concat_points = None

        if unnorm_box is not None:
            box_coords = unnorm_box.reshape(-1, 2, 2)
            box_labels = np.array([[2, 3]], dtype=np.float32)  # 修改为 float32
            box_labels = box_labels.repeat(unnorm_box.shape[0], 1)
            if concat_points is not None:
                concat_coords = np.concatenate([box_coords, concat_points[0]], axis=1)
                concat_labels = np.concatenate([box_labels, concat_points[1]], axis=1)
                concat_points = (concat_coords, concat_labels.astype(np.int32))
            else:
                concat_points = (box_coords, box_labels.astype(np.int32))

        if mask_input is None:
            mask_input_dummy = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)
            masks_enable = np.array([0], dtype=np.float32)
        else:
            mask_input_dummy = mask_input
            masks_enable = np.array([1], dtype=np.float32)

        if concat_points is None:
            raise ValueError("concat_points must be exists")

        orig_im_size = np.array(orig_hw, dtype=np.int32)

        sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings = self.decoder_session.run(
            None,
            {
                "point_coords": concat_points[0],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
                "point_labels": concat_points[1],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
                "mask_input": mask_input_dummy,   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
                "has_mask_input": masks_enable,   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
                "orig_im_size": orig_im_size,     # 添加模型期望的输入 i32
                "image_embed": features["image_embed"],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
                "high_res_feats_0": features["high_res_feats_0"],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
                "high_res_feats_1": features["high_res_feats_1"]   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
            }
        )

        masks, iou_pred = sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings

        low_res_masks = masks[:, 1:, :, :]
        iou_predictions = iou_pred[:, 1:]

        masks = self.postprocess_masks(masks, orig_hw)

        return masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks

    def transform_coords(self, coords, orig_hw):
        """
        转换坐标
        :param coords: 坐标
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 转换后的坐标
        """
        h, w = orig_hw
        coords = coords.copy()
        coords[..., 0] = coords[..., 0] / w
        coords[..., 1] = coords[..., 1] / h

        resolution = 1024
        coords = coords * resolution
        return coords

    def transform_boxes(self, boxes, orig_hw):
        """
        转换边框
        :param boxes: 边框
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 转换后的边框
        """
        boxes = self.transform_coords(boxes.reshape(-1, 2, 2), orig_hw)
        return boxes

    def postprocess_masks(self, masks, orig_hw):
        """
        后处理分割掩码
        :param masks: 分割掩码
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 后处理后的分割掩码
        """
        interpolated_masks = []
        for mask in masks:
            # 打印 mask 的形状，便于调试
            print("Original mask shape:", mask.shape)

            # 确保 mask 的形状为 (num_masks, height, width)
            if len(mask.shape) == 4:  # 如果有 batch 维度，去掉 batch 维度
                mask = np.squeeze(mask, axis=0)
            if len(mask.shape) == 3:  # 如果已经是 (num_masks, height, width)，直接使用
                pass
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"Unexpected mask shape: {mask.shape}")

            # 转换为 (height, width, num_masks)
            mask = np.transpose(mask, (1, 2, 0))

            # 调整大小到原始图像尺寸
            resized_mask = cv2.resize(mask, (orig_hw[1], orig_hw[0]), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)

            # 转换回 (num_masks, height, width)
            resized_mask = np.transpose(resized_mask, (2, 0, 1))

            interpolated_masks.append(resized_mask)

        interpolated_masks = np.array(interpolated_masks)

        return interpolated_masks

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 输入图片路径
    input_image_path = "../assets/rgb1.png"
    # 输出图片路径
    output_image_path = "../result/segment_onnx.png"
    # 编码器模型路径
    encoder_model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.encoder.onnx"
    # 解码器模型路径
    decoder_model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.decoder.onnx"

    # 读取输入图片
    image = cv2.imread(input_image_path)
    image = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
    orig_hw = image.shape[:2]

    # 初始化 SAM2 图像分割预测器
    predictor = SAM2ImagePredictor(encoder_model_path, decoder_model_path)

    # 设置输入图像并获取编码器的特征
    features = predictor.set_image(image)

    # 进行图像分割预测
    masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks = predictor.predict(features, orig_hw)

    # 将分割掩码转换为可视化图像
    mask = masks[0, 0].astype(np.uint8) * 255
    mask = cv2.cvtColor(mask, cv2.COLOR_GRAY2BGR)

    # 将分割掩码叠加到原始图像上
    result = cv2.addWeighted(image, 0.5, mask, 0.5, 0)
    result = cv2.cvtColor(result, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)

    # 保存输出图片
    cv2.imwrite(output_image_path, result)
```

## 回答
这个错误是由于在 `postprocess_masks` 函数中对 `resized_mask` 进行转置操作时，指定的轴与数组的形状不匹配导致的。根据错误信息 `ValueError: axes don't match array`，我们需要检查转置操作的轴参数。

在 `postprocess_masks` 函数中，`resized_mask` 的形状为 `(height, width, num_masks)`，当我们执行 `resized_mask = np.transpose(resized_mask, (2, 0, 1))` 时，期望 `resized_mask` 的形状为 `(num_masks, height, width)`。但根据错误信息，`resized_mask` 的形状可能不是预期的形状。

我们可以在转置操作之前添加一些打印语句，以检查 `resized_mask` 的形状。修改后的 `postprocess_masks` 函数如下：

```python
def postprocess_masks(self, masks, orig_hw):
    interpolated_masks = []
    for mask in masks:
        # 打印 mask 的形状，便于调试
        print("Original mask shape:", mask.shape)

        # 确保 mask 的形状为 (num_masks, height, width)
        if len(mask.shape) == 4:  # 如果有 batch 维度，去掉 batch 维度
            mask = np.squeeze(mask, axis=0)
        if len(mask.shape) == 3:  # 如果已经是 (num_masks, height, width)，直接使用
            pass
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unexpected mask shape: {mask.shape}")

        # 转换为 (height, width, num_masks)
        mask = np.transpose(mask, (1, 2, 0))

        # 调整大小到原始图像尺寸
        resized_mask = cv2.resize(mask, (orig_hw[1], orig_hw[0]), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)

        # 打印 resized_mask 的形状，便于调试
        print("Resized mask shape:", resized_mask.shape)

        # 转换回 (num_masks, height, width)
        resized_mask = np.transpose(resized_mask, (2, 0, 1))

        interpolated_masks.append(resized_mask)

    interpolated_masks = np.array(interpolated_masks)

    return interpolated_masks
```

通过添加 `print("Resized mask shape:", resized_mask.shape)`，我们可以查看 `resized_mask` 的形状，以确保它符合预期。根据打印结果，我们可以进一步调整代码以修复转置操作的轴参数。

如果 `resized_mask` 的形状不是预期的 `(height, width, num_masks)`，我们需要检查 `cv2.resize` 函数的参数是否正确。确保 `cv2.resize` 函数的输出形状符合预期。

如果 `resized_mask` 的形状正确，但转置操作仍然失败，我们需要检查 `resized_mask` 的维度顺序是否与预期一致。可以通过打印 `resized_mask.shape` 来确认维度顺序。

根据上述检查和修正，代码应该能够正确处理分割掩码的后处理操作。

## 回答
根据你给出的打印结果，`resized_mask` 的形状是 `(480, 640)`，这表明它是二维的，而代码里在转置时假定它是三维的 `(height, width, num_masks)` 形状，这就造成了轴不匹配的错误。

可以在调整大小之后对 `resized_mask` 的形状进行检查，若它是二维的，就添加一个新的维度，从而让它变为三维的。以下是修改后的 `postprocess_masks` 函数：

```python
def postprocess_masks(self, masks, orig_hw):
    interpolated_masks = []
    for mask in masks:
        # 打印 mask 的形状，便于调试
        print("Original mask shape:", mask.shape)

        # 确保 mask 的形状为 (num_masks, height, width)
        if len(mask.shape) == 4:  # 如果有 batch 维度，去掉 batch 维度
            mask = np.squeeze(mask, axis=0)
        if len(mask.shape) == 3:  # 如果已经是 (num_masks, height, width)，直接使用
            pass
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unexpected mask shape: {mask.shape}")

        # 转换为 (height, width, num_masks)
        mask = np.transpose(mask, (1, 2, 0))

        # 调整大小到原始图像尺寸
        resized_mask = cv2.resize(mask, (orig_hw[1], orig_hw[0]), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)

        # 打印 resized_mask 的形状，便于调试
        print("Resized mask shape:", resized_mask.shape)

        # 如果 resized_mask 是二维的，添加一个新的维度
        if len(resized_mask.shape) == 2:
            resized_mask = resized_mask[:, :, np.newaxis]

        # 转换回 (num_masks, height, width)
        resized_mask = np.transpose(resized_mask, (2, 0, 1))

        interpolated_masks.append(resized_mask)

    interpolated_masks = np.array(interpolated_masks)

    return interpolated_masks

```

下面是完整修改后的代码：

```python
# 测试SAM2.1图片分割

# 编码模型: ../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.encoder.onnx
## 输出: 
## high_res_feats_0
## name: high_res_feats_0
## tensor: float32[Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_0,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_1,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_2,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_3]
## high_res_feats_1
## name: high_res_feats_1
## tensor: float32[Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_0,Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_1,Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_2,Reshapehigh_res_feats_1_dim_3]
## image_embed
## name: image_embed
## tensor: float32[Reshapeimage_embed_dim_0,Reshapeimage_embed_dim_1,Reshapeimage_embed_dim_2,Reshapeimage_embed_dim_3]

# 解码模型: ../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.decoder.onnx
## 输出:
## masks
## name: masks
## tensor: float32[Resizemasks_dim_0,Resizemasks_dim_1,Resizemasks_dim_2,Resizemasks_dim_3]
## iou_predictions
## name: iou_predictions
## tensor: float32[Resizemasks_dim_0,Whereiou_predictions_dim_1]

# 输入图片../assets/rgb1.png
# 输出../result/segment_onnx.png

import os
import cv2
import numpy as np
import onnxruntime as ort

class SAM2ImagePredictor:
    def __init__(self, encoder_model_path, decoder_model_path):
        """
        初始化 SAM2 图像分割预测器
        :param encoder_model_path: 编码器模型路径
        :param decoder_model_path: 解码器模型路径
        """
        self.encoder_session = ort.InferenceSession(encoder_model_path)
        self.decoder_session = ort.InferenceSession(decoder_model_path)

    def trunc_normal(self, size, std=0.02, a=-2, b=2):
        """
        生成截断正态分布的随机数
        :param size: 输出数组的形状
        :param std: 标准差
        :param a: 截断下限
        :param b: 截断上限
        :return: 截断正态分布的随机数数组
        """
        values = np.random.normal(loc=0., scale=std, size=size)
        values = np.clip(values, a * std, b * std)
        return values

    def set_image(self, image):
        """
        设置输入图像并获取编码器的特征
        :param image: 输入图像
        :return: 编码器的特征
        """
        # 调整图像大小为模型期望的尺寸
        image = cv2.resize(image, (1024, 1024))
        # 调整通道顺序为模型期望的顺序（CHW）
        image = np.transpose(image, (2, 0, 1))
        # 添加批量维度
        image = np.expand_dims(image, axis=0).astype(np.float32)
        outputs = self.encoder_session.run(None, {"image": image})
        high_res_feats_0, high_res_feats_1, image_embed = outputs
        features = {
            "high_res_feats_0": high_res_feats_0,
            "high_res_feats_1": high_res_feats_1,
            "image_embed": image_embed
        }
        return features

    def predict(self, features, orig_hw, point_coords=None, point_labels=None, box=None, mask_input=None):
        """
        进行图像分割预测
        :param features: 编码器的特征
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :param point_coords: 点提示的坐标
        :param point_labels: 点提示的标签
        :param box: 边框提示
        :param mask_input: 掩码输入
        :return: 分割掩码、IoU 预测值和低分辨率掩码
        """
        if point_coords is not None and len(point_coords) != 0:
            point_coords = point_coords.astype(np.float32)
            unnorm_coords = self.transform_coords(point_coords, orig_hw)
            labels = point_labels.astype(np.float32)
            if len(unnorm_coords.shape) == 2:
                unnorm_coords, labels = unnorm_coords[None, ...], labels[None, ...]
        else:
            unnorm_coords, labels = None, None

        if box is not None:
            box = box.astype(np.float32)
            unnorm_box = self.transform_boxes(box, orig_hw)
        else:
            unnorm_box = None

        if mask_input is not None:
            mask_input = mask_input.astype(np.float32)
            if len(mask_input.shape) == 3:
                mask_input = mask_input[None, :, :, :]
        else:
            # 确保 mask_input 的维度为 4
            mask_input = np.zeros((1, 1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)

        # 如果没有点提示和边框提示，创建一个默认的点提示
        if unnorm_coords is None and unnorm_box is None:
            unnorm_coords = np.array([[[0.5, 0.5]]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认点提示在图像中心
            labels = np.array([[1]], dtype=np.float32)  # 默认标签为1，修改为 float32

        if unnorm_coords is not None:
            concat_points = (unnorm_coords, labels)
        else:
            concat_points = None

        if unnorm_box is not None:
            box_coords = unnorm_box.reshape(-1, 2, 2)
            box_labels = np.array([[2, 3]], dtype=np.float32)  # 修改为 float32
            box_labels = box_labels.repeat(unnorm_box.shape[0], 1)
            if concat_points is not None:
                concat_coords = np.concatenate([box_coords, concat_points[0]], axis=1)
                concat_labels = np.concatenate([box_labels, concat_points[1]], axis=1)
                concat_points = (concat_coords, concat_labels.astype(np.int32))
            else:
                concat_points = (box_coords, box_labels.astype(np.int32))

        if mask_input is None:
            mask_input_dummy = np.zeros((1, 256, 256), dtype=np.float32)
            masks_enable = np.array([0], dtype=np.float32)
        else:
            mask_input_dummy = mask_input
            masks_enable = np.array([1], dtype=np.float32)

        if concat_points is None:
            raise ValueError("concat_points must be exists")

        orig_im_size = np.array(orig_hw, dtype=np.int32)

        sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings = self.decoder_session.run(
            None,
            {
                "point_coords": concat_points[0],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
                "point_labels": concat_points[1],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
                "mask_input": mask_input_dummy,   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
                "has_mask_input": masks_enable,   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
                "orig_im_size": orig_im_size,     # 添加模型期望的输入 i32
                "image_embed": features["image_embed"],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
                "high_res_feats_0": features["high_res_feats_0"],  # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
                "high_res_feats_1": features["high_res_feats_1"]   # 修改为模型期望的输入名称 f32
            }
        )

        masks, iou_pred = sparse_embeddings, dense_embeddings

        low_res_masks = masks[:, 1:, :, :]
        iou_predictions = iou_pred[:, 1:]

        masks = self.postprocess_masks(masks, orig_hw)

        return masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks

    def transform_coords(self, coords, orig_hw):
        """
        转换坐标
        :param coords: 坐标
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 转换后的坐标
        """
        h, w = orig_hw
        coords = coords.copy()
        coords[..., 0] = coords[..., 0] / w
        coords[..., 1] = coords[..., 1] / h

        resolution = 1024
        coords = coords * resolution
        return coords

    def transform_boxes(self, boxes, orig_hw):
        """
        转换边框
        :param boxes: 边框
        :param orig_hw: 原始图像的高和宽
        :return: 转换后的边框
        """
        boxes = self.transform_coords(boxes.reshape(-1, 2, 2), orig_hw)
        return boxes

    def postprocess_masks(self, masks, orig_hw):
        interpolated_masks = []
        for mask in masks:
            # 打印 mask 的形状，便于调试
            print("Original mask shape:", mask.shape)

            # 确保 mask 的形状为 (num_masks, height, width)
            if len(mask.shape) == 4:  # 如果有 batch 维度，去掉 batch 维度
                mask = np.squeeze(mask, axis=0)
            if len(mask.shape) == 3:  # 如果已经是 (num_masks, height, width)，直接使用
                pass
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"Unexpected mask shape: {mask.shape}")

            # 转换为 (height, width, num_masks)
            mask = np.transpose(mask, (1, 2, 0))

            # 调整大小到原始图像尺寸
            resized_mask = cv2.resize(mask, (orig_hw[1], orig_hw[0]), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)

            # 打印 resized_mask 的形状，便于调试
            print("Resized mask shape:", resized_mask.shape)

            # 如果 resized_mask 是二维的，添加一个新的维度
            if len(resized_mask.shape) == 2:
                resized_mask = resized_mask[:, :, np.newaxis]

            # 转换回 (num_masks, height, width)
            resized_mask = np.transpose(resized_mask, (2, 0, 1))

            interpolated_masks.append(resized_mask)

        interpolated_masks = np.array(interpolated_masks)

        return interpolated_masks

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 输入图片路径
    input_image_path = "../assets/rgb1.png"
    # 输出图片路径
    output_image_path = "../result/segment_onnx.png"
    # 编码器模型路径
    encoder_model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.encoder.onnx"
    # 解码器模型路径
    decoder_model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.decoder.onnx"

    # 读取输入图片
    image = cv2.imread(input_image_path)
    image = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
    orig_hw = image.shape[:2]

    # 初始化 SAM2 图像分割预测器
    predictor = SAM2ImagePredictor(encoder_model_path, decoder_model_path)

    # 设置输入图像并获取编码器的特征
    features = predictor.set_image(image)

    # 进行图像分割预测
    masks, iou_predictions, low_res_masks = predictor.predict(features, orig_hw)

    # 将分割掩码转换为可视化图像
    mask = masks[0, 0].astype(np.uint8) * 255
    mask = cv2.cvtColor(mask, cv2.COLOR_GRAY2BGR)

    # 将分割掩码叠加到原始图像上
    result = cv2.addWeighted(image, 0.5, mask, 0.5, 0)
    result = cv2.cvtColor(result, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)

    # 保存输出图片
    cv2.imwrite(output_image_path, result)

```

你可以使用上述代码来避免 `axes don't match array` 错误。 

# 问题
参考代码:
```rs
#![allow(unused)]

//! 使用ort预测深度
//! 模型文件model_path = "../../../assets/ailia-models/depth_anything/depth_anything_v2_vits.onnx"
//! 待预测图片img_path = "../assets/rgb1.png"
//! 输出路径: ./result/ort_depth.png

// 导入标准库路径处理模块
use std::path::Path;

// 导入ORT相关模块
use ort::{inputs, session::Session};
use ort::value::Tensor;

// 图像处理相关模块
use image::{ImageBuffer, Luma};

// 错误处理模块
use anyhow::Result;

// 线性代数库
use ndarray::Array;

fn main() -> Result<()> {
    // 定义模型路径、输入图片路径和输出路径
    let model_path = "../../assets/ailia-models/depth_anything/depth_anything_v2_vits.onnx";
    let img_path = "./assets/rgb1.png";
    let output_path = "./result/ort_depth.png";

    // 创建输出目录（如果不存在）
    std::fs::create_dir_all(Path::new(output_path).parent().unwrap())?;

    // 创建ORT会话并加载模型
    let mut session = Session::builder()?.commit_from_file(model_path)?;

    // 加载输入图片并转换为RGB格式
    let img = image::open(img_path)?;
    
    // 将图片缩放到模型要求的518x518分辨率
    let resized_img = img.resize_exact(518, 518, image::imageops::FilterType::Nearest);

    // 创建四维数组保存预处理数据 [batch=1, channels=3, height=518, width=518]
    let mut input_array = Array::zeros((1, 3, 518, 518));

    // 遍历所有像素进行归一化处理
    for (x, y, pixel) in resized_img.to_rgb8().enumerate_pixels() {
        // 将RGB通道值归一化到0-1范围
        input_array[[0, 0, y as usize, x as usize]] = pixel[0] as f32 / 255.0;
        input_array[[0, 1, y as usize, x as usize]] = pixel[1] as f32 / 255.0;
        input_array[[0, 2, y as usize, x as usize]] = pixel[2] as f32 / 255.0;
    }

    // 将数组转换为ORT张量
    let input_tensor = ort::value::Value::from_array(input_array.into_dyn())?;

    // 运行模型推理
    let outputs = session.run(inputs![input_tensor])?;

    // 处理输出张量
    let output_tensor = outputs["select_36"]
        .try_extract_tensor::<f32>()?;
        
    let (shape , data) = output_tensor;
    let array = Array::from_shape_vec((518, 518), data.to_vec())?;
    
    // 计算深度值范围用于归一化
    let min_depth = array.iter().fold(f32::INFINITY, |a, &b| a.min(b));
    let max_depth = array.iter().fold(f32::NEG_INFINITY, |a, &b| a.max(b));
    

    // 创建灰度图像缓冲区
    let mut img_buffer: ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>> = ImageBuffer::new(518, 518);

    // 将深度值归一化到0-255范围并保存到灰度图像缓冲区
    for (x, y, pixel) in img_buffer.enumerate_pixels_mut() {
        let depth = array[[y as usize, x as usize]];
        let normalized_depth = ((depth - min_depth) / (max_depth - min_depth) * 255.0) as u8;
        *pixel = Luma([normalized_depth]);
    }

    // 保存深度图
    img_buffer.save(output_path)?;

    Ok(())
}
```

```py
# 测试SAM2.1图片分割

# 编码模型: ../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.encoder.onnx
## 输出: 
## high_res_feats_0
## name: high_res_feats_0
## tensor: float32[Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_0,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_1,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_2,Reshapehigh_res_feats_0_dim_3]

# 解码模型: ../../../assets/ailia-models/segment-anything-2/sam2.1_base_plus.decoder.onnx
```
